# Skills · C · part 216

909 entries, csp-graph-refactor to csv-catalog-normalizer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **csp-graph-refactor**: Graph-powered safe refactoring. Use when renaming symbols, moving code between modules, extracting functions, detecting dead code, or validating that a refactoring won't break dependents. Covers rename preview, dependency-safe moves, dead code detection, and post-refactor verification. ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-workflow/skills/csp-graph-refactor) · ★1)
- **csp-graph-review**: Graph-powered code review with minimal context assembly and risk scoring. Use when reviewing a PR or diff with graph support — assembles only the code the reviewer needs, scores risk structurally, and runs pre-merge checks. Enhances csp-code-review with structural awareness. ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-workflow/skills/csp-graph-review) · ★1)
- **csp-grilling**: Use when stress-testing a plan, design, or decision before implementation. Uses design-tree + frontier-based interviewing to map decisions and their dependencies, asking the whole frontier in each round until nothing is left unexamined. Inspired by mattpocock/skills grilling pattern. Keywords: grill, grilling, 盘问, 压力测试, design tree, 设计树, frontier, 决策审查. ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-meta/skills/csp-grilling) · ★1)
- **csp-h5-visual-testing**: H5/移动端页面自动化视觉走查与截图取证：启动开发服务器、注入 mock 数据，用 Playwright 执行批量截图、多 Tab 遍历、深浅色模式对比、交互录屏，生成标准 HTML 测试报告。 本技能只取证不修码——发现问题一律输出 FIX_SUGGESTION.md 等用户确认，禁止直接改项目代码。 当用户需要自动化截图、H5测试、H5截图、UI走查、视觉回归、深色模式截图、主题切换截图、 Tab分支截图、交互录屏、页面渲染验证时使用。务必在涉及 H5/移动端页面批量视觉取证时使用 此 skill，而不是手动起服务肉眼检查页面。 ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-patterns/skills/csp-h5-visual-testing) · ★1)
- **csp-handoff**: Use when approaching context limit (300K+ tokens), needing a clean session start, delegating work to another agent, or saving progress. Creates/loads a structured JSON handoff with goals, decisions, completed/pending steps, and file changes. Keywords: handoff, 交接, save state, resume, 继续, clean session, context rot. ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-runtime/skills/csp-handoff) · ★1)
- **csp-hardware-embedded**: Hardware and embedded engineering: device-cloud interface contracts first, then board-level architecture, component selection and BOM, firmware/driver task scheduling. Use when working on MCU/RTOS firmware, PCB and circuit review, device protocols (MQTT/Modbus/UART/BLE/CAN). ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-patterns/skills/csp-hardware-embedded) · ★1)
- **csp-headers**: Use when the user says 'CSP', 'Content-Security-Policy', 'security headers', 'HSTS', 'X-Frame-Options', 'clickjacking', 'XSS protection headers', or needs to configure HTTP security headers for a web application. Do NOT use for API endpoint security (see api-audit) or dependency scanning (see dependency-audit). ([jtucker9/mystuff](https://github.com/jtucker9/mystuff/tree/HEAD/skills/security/csp-headers))
- **csp-headers**: Use when designing or hardening a Content-Security-Policy header for an HTTP application. Applies when the user asks for "CSP", "Content Security Policy", "nonce-based CSP", "strict-dynamic", or "XSS hardening headers". Do not use for non-web platforms (native, mobile), for CORS configuration (use a CORS skill), or for setting CSP via a managed WAF without owning the header value. ([Mine-FNL/skillmd-lint](https://github.com/Mine-FNL/skillmd-lint/tree/HEAD/examples/csp-headers))
- **csp-hotfix**: CSP 热修复工作流 — 快速修复线上 bug 的轻量级流程。 跳过 design 阶段，直接进入 build → verify → archive。 适用于紧急 bug 修复、安全漏洞、线上故障。 Use when: 生产环境 bug、紧急安全漏洞、线上故障。 Keywords: hotfix, 热修复, 紧急修复, 线上bug, 生产问题, quick fix, emergency fix ([maythyai/code-skills-package](https://github.com/maythyai/code-skills-package/tree/HEAD/packages/csp-code/csp-workflow/skills/csp-hotfix) · ★1)

- **csp-i18n-testing**: Testing internationalization and localization: FormatJS CLI extract-diff for translation completeness CI gates, eslint-plugin-formatjs for ICU MessageFormat validation, Playwright multi-locale context for locale/timezone/RTL rendering, pseudo-localization (qps-ploc) to surface hardcoded strings without waiting for translations, and plural-rule/snapshot tests for date/number/currency formatting. Unlike csp-i18n-frameworks (setup) and csp-locale-management (formatting patterns), this skill wires i18n correctness into the automated test pipeline.
